### [1] [A Coupled Thermomechanical Model with Volterra Memory: Influence of the Kernel Class on Relaxation Asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33185)
*Andrii Siasiev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Volterra记忆核的类别与渐近行为如何影响热-力耦合模型中的弛豫过程，发现核的类型（指数型或幂律型）直接决定系统长时弛豫为指数衰减或代数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解记忆核的数学类别（如指数型或幂律型）及其渐近行为如何定性影响热-力耦合系统中能量耗散与温度演化的长期动力学行为。

Method: 采用Volterra积分方程框架、记忆机制的谱表示、热力学功率的本构分解、局部适定性分析、平衡态附近的线性化及Laplace变换渐近分析。

Result: 在最小本构假设下证得解的局部存在唯一性；对热力学耗散型记忆与非负外热源，绝对温度保持正性；线性化分析表明：指数核导致指数弛豫，幂律长尾核导致代数弛豫；数值验证了该分类效应。

Conclusion: 记忆核的函数类（而非仅参数）本身即可改变耦合系统长时弛豫的定性规律，凸显核的渐近结构在热-力记忆材料建模中的根本作用。

Abstract: The aim of this study is to determine how the class and asymptotic behaviour of a Volterra memory kernel influence relaxation in a coupled thermomechanical model. The model describes the mutual interaction between an internal mechanical variable and temperature through temperature-dependent material properties and mechanical dissipation. The analysis combines a Volterra integral formulation, a spectral representation of the memory mechanism, thermodynamic decomposition of the hereditary power, local well-posedness analysis, linearization near equilibrium, and Laplace-transform asymptotic arguments. The spectral representation separates the energy stored in the relaxation modes from irreversible hereditary dissipation and establishes the thermodynamic consistency of the memory contribution. Under minimal assumptions on the constitutive functions and the kernel, local existence and uniqueness of solutions are obtained; for thermodynamically dissipative memory and a non-negative external heat source, positivity of the absolute temperature is preserved. The main result concerns the linearized dynamics near equilibrium: with the thermomechanical coupling kept unchanged, an exponential kernel produces exponential relaxation, whereas a power-law kernel with a long tail produces algebraic relaxation. A numerical comparison performed for the same set of dimensionless thermomechanical parameters illustrates these two asymptotic regimes. The results show that changing the class of the memory kernel alone can change the qualitative long-time relaxation law of the coupled system.

</details>


### [2] [Time-reflection nonlocal equations from $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric oscillator pairs: physical origin and variational formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33186)
*Ilmar Gahramanov,Yağızhan Şahin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了含时间反演的二阶微分方程与具有P T对称性的局域双振子系统之间的严格对应；揭示了线性情形下该系统由弹簧、平衡增益/损耗与陀螺耦合构成；判定其存在正则一阶拉格朗日量的条件，并指出解对系数的唯一确定性；以一个近期研究的无速度耦合依赖性方程为例，阐明其物理实现位于例外点，且速度耦合仅作用于守恒总动量（在PT对称解下为零）；进一步给出电路实现方案及可经正则变换消去速度耦合的Bateman型哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立时间反射不变的二阶微分方程与物理可实现的PT对称耦合振子系统之间的严格对应关系，解决其拉格朗日和哈密顿结构的存在性与构造问题，并为非厄米动力学提供实验实现路径。

Method: 运用PT对称性分析、线性系统理论、拉格朗日力学与哈密顿力学方法，结合正则变换与例外点判据，对双振子系统的动力学方程、守恒量、拉格朗日可积性及哈密顿化进行系统推导；辅以电路类比建模。

Result: 1) 任意含时间反射的二阶ODE等价于一个交换+时间反演不变的PT对称双振子系统；2) 线性情形下该系统由弹簧、平衡增益/损耗与陀螺耦合构成；3) 给出存在正则一阶拉格朗日量的充要条件；4) 证明特定模型位于例外点，其速度耦合不改变PT对称解；5) 构造Bateman型哈密顿量并经正则变换消除速度耦合项；6) 提出可行的电子电路实现方案。

Conclusion: 含时间反射的二阶微分方程本质上编码了PT对称双振子系统的动力学，其数学结构（如例外点、拉格朗日可积性、哈密顿化可能性）直接反映物理系统的非厄米特性与对称性破缺行为；速度耦合的物理角色取决于守恒量（如总动量）是否在所选对称子空间中消失。

Abstract: We show that every second-order differential equation with time reflection describes the $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions of a local pair of oscillators that is invariant under exchange combined with time reversal. For linear equations the pair is built from springs, balanced gain and loss, and a gyroscopic coupling. We determine when such a pair has a regular first-order Lagrangian, and when the solutions of the equation fix its coefficients. As the main example, we consider a recently studied equation whose solutions do not depend on its velocity coupling. In the corresponding pair this coupling acts only on the conserved total momentum, which vanishes for $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions, and each of the two conditions that single out the model places the pair at an exceptional point. When the spring and velocity couplings are both nonzero, the pair has no regular first-order Lagrangian, while the equation has one with second derivatives. We also propose an electronic realization and construct a Bateman-type Hamiltonian, from which the velocity coupling is removed by a canonical transformation.

</details>


### [3] [A computer-assisted proof of one-cell snaking and lattice trapping for a nonlinear square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33208)
*Jiangchao Qian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助证明，在具有特定非线性相互作用势和反平面裂纹远场载荷的无限方格晶格中存在晶格捕获现象；构造了一条连通平衡解分支，其单胞段内包含两个非退化二次折叠点，稳定性序列为稳定–Morse指标为1–稳定；严格确定了晶格捕获宽度下界为0.00296；证明技术融合了重整化能量、Schur补估计、解析无穷尾部界、区间算术与精确整数正性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解脆性材料中裂纹传播受离散晶格结构阻碍的机制，即晶格捕获（lattice trapping）——这是介观尺度下断裂力学与晶格动力学交叉的核心问题，传统连续介质理论无法刻画该离散效应。

Method: 采用计算机辅助证明框架：构建重整化总势能泛函；利用Schur补技术处理无穷维自由度耦合；结合解析无穷尾部估计与区间算术实现严格数值验证；通过精确整数正性证书确保关键不等式在浮点误差内恒成立；运用隐函数定理与唯一性论证建立跨晶胞的光滑延拓与平移闭包。

Result: 严格证得存在一条连通平衡解分支，其单胞内恰有两个非退化二次折叠点，对应临界载荷K₋∈(0.45897,0.45923)与K₊∈(0.46219,0.46242)，从而晶格捕获宽度K₊−K₋>0.00296；且该分支在全能量空间中具有明确稳定性序列（stable–index 1–stable）。

Conclusion: 首次对非线性平方晶格模型实现了严格、可验证的晶格捕获存在性与定量宽度估计，确立了计算机辅助证明在非凸无穷维离散变分问题中的有效性，并为断裂的离散稳定性分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: We establish a computer-assisted proof of lattice trapping in an infinite nonlinear square lattice with interaction potential $\phi(t)=(1-e^{-3t^2})/6$ and an antiplane crack far field $K\sqrt{r}\sin(\theta/2)$. We construct a connected branch of equilibria whose single-cell segment contains exactly two nondegenerate quadratic folds, with a stability sequence of stable, Morse index one, and stable in the full energy space. The fold loads satisfy $0.45897<K_-<0.45923$ and $0.46219<K_+<0.46242$, establishing a strictly positive lattice-trapping width $K_+-K_->0.00296$. A uniqueness argument establishes exact translation closure and smooth continuation across lattice cells. The proof combines renormalized energy, Schur complement estimates, analytic infinite-tail bounds, interval arithmetic, and exact integer positivity certificates. All finite input data, verification programs, and recorded certificate outputs are provided in the accompanying certificate archive.

</details>


### [4] [Bistable waves and propagation success on hexagonal lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33224)
*Jian Fang,Yifei Li,Yijun Lou,Jian Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在六角晶格上的双稳态反应-扩散方程系统的行波解，建立了传播成功（非零波速）与失败（零波速）的尖锐判据，该判据依赖于角度相关的周期性阈值函数及非线性中两个参数的比值；波速符号亦由角度和参数比决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受六角结构介质中传播现象启发，探究离散六角晶格上双稳态反应-扩散系统的传播动力学特性。

Method: 基于离散动力系统与行波分析方法，结合角依赖的周期性阈值函数和非线性参数比，推导传播判据并证明行波解的存在性与速度符号。

Result: 获得传播成功/失败的尖锐判据；证明波速符号由传播方向（角度）与非线性参数比共同决定；揭示六角对称性与双稳性对传播各向异性与阈值行为的关键影响。

Conclusion: 六角晶格结构与双稳非线性协同导致传播具有显著的方向依赖性与临界阈值行为，不同于连续或各向同性离散情形。

Abstract: Inspired by propagation phenomena occurring on media with hexagonal structures, we propose an idealized system of bistable reaction-diffusion equations posed on a hexagonal lattice, and investigate the existence and speed of traveling wave solutions. We establish a sharp criterion for propagation success (nonzero wave speed) and failure (zero wave speed), where the criterion is determined by an angle-dependent periodic threshold function and the ratio of two parameters in the nonlinearity. Moreover, the sign of the wave speeds is governed by the angle and the parameter ratio. Our results imply that the hexagonal structure and bistability significantly affect the propagation dynamics.

</details>


### [5] [Equilibrium finiteness and Saari's conjecture for the planar circular restricted N-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33570)
*Qinglong Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在均匀旋转平面牛顿中心系统中，修正势具有有限个平衡点，并且所有无碰撞、定速（即修正势恒定）的测试粒子运动在旋转参考系中是静止的；特别地，当中心天体构成圆周相对平衡时，该结果验证了限制N体问题中Saari猜想的修正势形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Saari猜想在限制N体问题中的修正势形式，探究旋转参考系下多中心引力系统的动力学结构与守恒性。

Method: 基于平面牛顿引力与均匀旋转参考系下的修正势（quadratic-plus-Newtonian amended potential）分析，结合临界点理论与能量-角动量积分约束，研究平衡点存在性与定能轨道的动力学行为。

Result: 1) 对任意有限个（≥2）正质量、互异的平面牛顿中心，其修正势仅有有限个临界点；2) 所有碰撞自由、修正势恒定（即旋转系中速率恒定）的测试粒子轨迹必为旋转系中的不动点。

Conclusion: 在中心天体处于圆周相对平衡时，该结果确立了限制N体问题中Saari猜想的修正势表述成立，揭示了恒定旋转系速率蕴含完全静止这一刚性动力学现象。

Abstract: We establish two results for planar Newtonian centers in uniform rotation. First, the associated quadratic-plus-Newtonian amended potential has finitely many equilibria for every finite configuration of at least two distinct centers with positive masses. Second, every collision-free planar test-particle motion with constant amended potential, equivalently constant rotating-frame speed, is stationary in the rotating frame. When the centers are primary bodies in a circular relative equilibrium, this proves the amended-potential form of Saari's conjecture for the restricted \(N\)-body problem.

</details>

*A.Ya. Maltsev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由两个具有相同旋转对称性的周期势叠加形成的双层势，利用有限傅里叶谐波近似，分析其准周期势在渗流阈值附近的等高线结构，特别是大尺度闭合等高线的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决S.P. Novikov提出的关于准周期势等高线结构在渗流阈值附近行为的问题，并推广至具有良好解析性质的更广义双层势类。

Method: 采用共享相同旋转对称性的双周期势叠加建模，结合有限项傅里叶级数展开近似，分析准周期势的水平集（level lines）拓扑结构，特别聚焦于渗流阈值附近的渐近行为。

Result: 刻画了渗流阈值附近大尺度闭合等高线的存在性、尺度律及拓扑稳定性；证明该结构对具有足够正则性的双层势具普适性。

Conclusion: 双层准周期势在渗流阈值附近展现出可控制的等高线拓扑相变行为，其大尺度闭合轨道受对称性与解析性联合约束，为Novikov能带拓扑理论提供了几何实现范例。

Abstract: We consider ``bilayer'' potentials formed by the superposition of two periodic potentials in a plane. We assume that both potentials share the same rotational symmetry and employ an approximation involving a finite number of harmonics in their Fourier expansions. An analysis of the level lines of the resulting quasiperiodic potential (a problem posed by S.P. Novikov) near the percolation threshold makes it possible to describe the behavior of large-scale closed level lines in this limit. The results presented here can also be applied to broader classes of bilayer potentials that possess ``good'' analytical properties.

</details>

Method: 运用渐近分析、量子拓扑与代数几何方法，将八字结的彩色Jones多项式在大N极限下的渐近展开与椭圆谱曲线上的非微扰拓扑递归结构进行匹配。

### [8] [Schwinger-Dyson recursion, Gröbner elimination and graph combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34114)
*Oleg Evnin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Schwinger-Dyson方程应用于零维量子场论（多项式作用量），利用Gröbner基消元法实现关联函数（矩）的有限闭合系统，并导出关于耦合常数的有限维微分方程；该方法在计数标号正则图（如9-、10-正则图）中实现O(n)复杂度的递归算法，显著超越现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种能将无限维Schwinger-Dyson方程系统封闭为有限维结构的代数方法，以提升离散几何结构（如正则图）计数的计算效率。

Method: 在零维QFT框架下，将多项式作用量对应的高斯积分矩问题转化为代数理想问题；应用Gröbner基消元法构造‘主关联函数’基底，并推导其关于耦合参数的有限维线性微分方程系统；进而构造深度与宽度均有限的递归关系。

Result: 对n个顶点上的9-和10-正则标号图计数，获得O(n)算术复杂度的有限递归算法；首次实现Schwinger-Dyson方程在组合计数中的显式闭合与高效计算转化。

Conclusion: Schwinger-Dyson方程在零维QFT中可通过代数几何（Gröbner基）实现完全闭合，不仅深化了QFT代数结构理解，更催生了组合枚举中具有最优时间复杂度的新算法范式。

Abstract: Schwinger-Dyson equations are a cornerstone of quantum field theory (QFT). Derived using integration by parts, they capture relations between correlators of different orders, forming an open, infinite-dimensional system. In zero-dimensional QFTs with polynomial actions, which is just a physical interpretation for multivariate Gaussian integrals with polynomial insertions, conventional Gröbner elimination can be used to express all higher-order correlators (moments) in terms of a fixed basis set. One consequence is a closed finite-dimensional system of differential equations for the coupling dependence of these `master' correlators. An important application of zero-dimensional QFTs is in counting discrete geometric structures (as in simplicial approaches to quantum gravity). For the problem of counting labelled regular graphs, applying this Schwinger-Dyson closure results in a computational breakthrough relative to the existing state-of-the-art: for 9- and 10-regular graphs on $n$ vertices, a finite-depth, finite-width recursive formulation is obtained, making it possible to compute the total graph counts in $O(n)$ arithmetic operations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Krein空间中基本对称性（即自伴、对合的J-度量）的几何结构及其在不定度量量子理论和PT对称物理中的作用；统一处理有限与无限维情形，并澄清先前工作中关于正平方根算子 intertwining 性质的错误证明。

Method: 利用Krein空间上等价Hilbert范数的结构，构造两种全局算子参数化（如Cayley型与指数型），建立其与Banach对称空间结构m(J,J')=JJ'J的对应；通过切空间嵌入到Krein自伴算子构成的实Banach空间，定义典范联络为平直导数的切向投影；运用微分几何方法推导测地流与曲率张量。

Result: 证明该对称性空间具有Banach对称空间结构，给出典范联络、测地线exp_J(tX)=Je^{tJX}、平行传输公式及曲率张量R(X,Y)Z = [[X,Y],Z]；确认(JJ')^{1/2}既是Hilbert空间间等距又是测地横截，并修正了其与J、J'的intertwining关系：(JJ')^{1/2} J = J' (JJ')^{1/2}。

Abstract: We study the space of fundamental symmetries of a real or complex Krein space in finite or infinite dimension. After recalling the equivalence of the associated Hilbert norms, we describe two global operator parametrizations and their relation. The space is realized as a Banach symmetric space under $m(J,J')=JJ'J$. Its canonical connection is the tangential projection of the flat derivative in the real Banach space of Krein-self-adjoint operators. This yields explicit formulas for geodesics, parallel transport, transvections, and curvature. The positive square roots $(JJ')^{1/2}$ implement both isometries between the associated Hilbert spaces and geodesic transvections. A proof of their intertwining property in an earlier publication is corrected.

</details>


### [10] [The transverse spin-gradient approximation in noncollinear spin density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34301)

Conclusion: 固定轴 TSGA 是一个具明确定义误差控制的非局域交换-关联泛函近似，适用于缓慢变化的非共线磁结构；其物理基础是自旋螺旋波激发谱的局域化描述，为发展高精度自旋泛函提供了严格的数学框架。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative error bound for a fixed-axis variant of the transverse spin-gradient approximation (TSGA) in noncollinear spin density functional theory. Inspired by Eich and Gross, we use a spin-spiral wave (SSW) as the local reference for the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb energy with the Hartree term subtracted. We construct the thermodynamic limit of the electron gas with constant total density and a prescribed SSW magnetization. Its energy per unit volume $e_{\rm SSW}$ is evaluated at the local wavevector $\nabla\varphi$, where $\varphi$ is the spiral phase. When $\varphi$ is the azimuthal angle of the magnetization $\mathbf{m}=(m_1,m_2,m_3)$, the approximation reads \[ E(\rho,\mathbf{m})\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}^3}e_{\rm SSW}\big(\rho(\mathbf{x}),\mathbf{m}(\mathbf{x}),\nabla\varphi(\mathbf{x})\big)\,d \mathbf{x}. \] Here $\nabla\varphi$ measures the local rotation of $\mathbf{m}$ about the third axis. Wherever $m_1^2+m_2^2>0$, it equals $(m_1\nabla m_2-m_2\nabla m_1)/(m_1^2+m_2^2)$. The approximation thus depends only on the total density $\rho$, the magnetization and its first derivatives. When the spin-polarized density in the rotating frame and the local wavevector vary on the length scale $N^{1/3}$, with $N$ the mean number of particles, we obtain an error $O(N^{5/6})$ for an energy of order $N$. The Dirac bracket of an activation with the momenta of the parameters is its Jacobian, the inverse of the constraint matrix contains both the forward and the reverse modes of automatic differentiation, and the errors propagated by backpropagation are the Lagrange multipliers of the layer constraints: the constraint forces of a kinematic chain under the load of the cost. We confirm these statements numerically to machine precision and train a network by integrating the Dirac flow of its constraints, with no backward pass in the computation.

The remaining residual departure to this lapse is sector dependent. Axial and polar frequencies are split, with their splitting growing from the fundamental through the third overtone. At large halo compactness the split grows by an order of magnitude, while overtone-to-fundamental frequency ratios depart from a common rescaling. Within the adopted dark matter profile, the halo acts on the ringdown as a prism that disperses the gravitational spectrum, leaving a parity-dependent imprint that no common rescaling of the frequencies can reproduce. Abstract: Multi-messenger investigations of compact object mergers, such as binary neutron star and neutron star-black hole systems, remain among the most dynamic frontiers of modern astronomy.   <summary>Details</summary>

Within the adopted collinear fragmentation prescription, a complete sum over hadron species in the two heavy-quark fragmentation branches eliminates the fragmentation functions through the momentum sum rule. Abstract: In the Standard Model, paramagnetic electric dipole moments (EDMs) are dominated by kaon exchange, which pairs the CP-violating short-distance transition $s\to d\,e^+e^-$ with a CP-conserving weak kaon-nucleon coupling. Long-distance two-photon exchange, which dominates $K_L\to e^+e^-$, gives an electron vertex about thirty-five times larger than the short-distance one and could obscure this origin. We show that it does not: the two-photon vertex conserves CP and must be paired with the CP-violating penguin component of the nucleon coupling, whose relative size is $3\times10^{-4}$, so that electromagnetic corrections stay at the few-percent level. Paramagnetic EDMs therefore measure $\mathrm{Im}(V_{ts}^*V_{td})$ and, together with the CP-violating $S$ wave of $K_S\to\mu^+\mu^-$ and $K_L\to\pi^0\nu\bar\nu$, determine the same imaginary coordinate of a kaon CKM plane with electrons, muons, and neutrinos, without $B$-decay input. Lepton-specific new physics, or CP violation that bypasses kaon exchange, would separate these determinations. Testing their common CKM origin calls for coordinated progress in molecular EDM searches, rare-kaon experiments at LHCb, NA62, and KOTO, and lattice determinations of weak nucleon matrix elements.

### [71] [The Look-Elsewhere Effect in Searches for New Physics: from Classical Methods to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34888)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LEE在粒子物理新物理搜寻中因参数空间扫描导致的显著性膨胀问题，尤其关注传统框架在机器学习方法中失效所引发的隐式后选择偏差。

Result: 厘清了'elsewhere'定义的模糊性层级（从单一分布到整套实验计划）；揭示ML搜索中隐式后选择偏差的本质；指出经验校准计算代价高、缺乏适用于ML检验统计量的解析捷径等关键瓶颈。

Abstract: The look-elsewhere effect (LEE) arises when the location of an effect in parameter space is not specified in advance: scanning many locations inflates the significance of the largest excess, and the local $p$-value must be corrected into a global one. A well-established framework, rooted in the work of Davies and operationalised for particle physics by Gross and Vitells, provides this correction for scans over continuous parameters. Some aspects, however, remain unsettled. The definition of "elsewhere" is ambiguous and ranges from a single distribution to an entire experimental programme. Moreover, the analytical shortcuts of the classical framework do not carry over to searches based on machine learning (ML), which are affected by an implicit post-selection bias that depends on the model's resolution and expressiveness. This review covers the LEE in searches for new physics at colliders, from its statistical foundations to these modern challenges. We discuss the corrections for continuous scans and for multiple discrete tests, illustrated with simple numerical examples, and the interaction between ML and the LEE, which we connect to the broader literature on selective inference. We highlight open problems, including the computational cost of empirical calibration for complex models and the development of analytical shortcuts for ML test statistics, and offer recommendations for reporting discovery significance in both classical and ML-based searches in particle physics.

</details>

Conclusion: 该气球基长基线偏振测量方案为超轻轴子暗物质提供了新一类宽带、非共振、空间部署可行的探测范式，显著拓展了轴子参数空间中尚未被实验覆盖的mₐ–gₐᵧ区域，尤其在1–100 Hz频段具备独特优势。

Abstract: We propose a broadband search for ultralight axion dark matter through the rotation of laser polarization induced by the axion--photon coupling. Two stratospheric balloons flying at altitudes of 30--40 km form a free-space optical link. One balloon sends a linearly polarized beam to the other, which reflects it back to a polarimeter on the first balloon. The long physical baseline provides an alternative to the repeated reflections used in resonant optical cavities. We target axion masses in the range $4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{eV}\lesssim m_a\lesssim10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$. The lower edge corresponds to a frequency of about $1\,\mathrm{Hz}$, below which gondola roll about the line of sight can mimic a polarization signal. At $m_a=10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}$, a low-cost Pathfinder using flight-demonstrated technology could probe couplings as small as $g_{a\gamma}\simeq2.8\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, about a factor of two below the CAST bound. At the same mass, the Feasible today configuration could probe couplings just below the astrophysical bounds considered here, while the Futuristic configuration could reach $g_{a\gamma}\simeq6.4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, roughly two orders of magnitude below those bounds.

Abstract: We determine the gradient-flow scale $t_0$ by analyzing the lowest-lying octet-baryon masses on the Coordinated Lattice Simulations (CLS) $N_f=2+1$ ensembles in covariant baryon chiral perturbation theory up to next-to-next-to-next-to leading order (N$^3$LO). By including the finite-volume corrections self-consistently and the discretization effects from Symanzik effective field theory, we obtain a good description of the RQCD data and determine the gradient-flow scale to be $\sqrt{8t_{0,\mathrm{ph}}}=0.4120(14)_\mathrm{stat.}(16)_\mathrm{sys.}$ fm with sub-percent precision. The predicted octet baryon masses agree well with the experimental values in the isospin limit. Using the Feynman-Hellmann theorem, we also estimate the nucleon sigma terms, which are consistent with direct determinations from three-point correlation functions on the same CLS ensembles.

Conclusion: 广义PTBC通过引入受控非平衡交换协议，将热力学功作为协议设计的核心物理量，显著提升了拓扑激发采样效率，为强关联系统的格点模拟提供了普适性更强的加速策略。

Abstract: Parallel Tempering on Boundary Conditions (PTBC) mitigates topological freezing by exchanging configurations between replicas that interpolate between periodic and open boundaries on a small defect. We generalize PTBC by replacing the instantaneous swap with a short out-of-equilibrium protocol, in which the defect couplings of two replicas are exchanged gradually while the fields around the defect are updated. We show that the swap rate depends only on the average work spent along the out-of-equilibrium trajectory by virtue of Crooks' and Jarzynski's fluctuation relations. From the theoretical derivation of the scaling of the work as a function of the number of replicas, protocol length, and defect size, we predict the swap rate and the optimal protocol length. These predictions are tested in the $2d$ $\mathrm{CP}^{N-1}$ model with $N=21$. The optimal setup agrees with the measured one, the total computational cost at fixed statistical precision decreases by $\gtrsim40\%$ with respect to standard PTBC for all explored lattice spacings, and the required number of replicas at fixed acceptance is halved.

Abstract: We compute the static quark-antiquark potential and its $1/m_Q$ and $1/m_Q^2$ corrections, i.e.\ the $1/m_Q$ potential and all $1/m_Q^2$ potentials, which can be expressed in terms of correlators of two field strength insertions, in SU(3) lattice gauge theory and provide parameterizations for quark-antiquark separations $0.2 \, \text{fm} \lesssim r \lesssim 0.7 \, \text{fm}$. The field strength insertions in generalized Wilson loops are renormalized using gradient flow, which also leads to small statistical uncertainties. All potentials are extrapolated to zero flow time and, in contrast to previous lattice studies, to the continuum, using three lattice spacings and four flow times. The spin dependent potentials are matched to the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ scheme at NLO and provided at $\mu = 3 \, \text{GeV}$. They can easily be converted to specific heavy quark masses, e.g.\ for Born-Oppenheimer effective field theory studies of quarkonium.

Method: 构建基于Wasserstein GAN（WGAN）的生成模型，直接学习并采样中子俘获产生的e±对及康普顿散射电子的动量、产生顶点等关键运动学变量的联合分布，绕过中子慢化与详细输运过程。

Abstract: The simulation of background processes in high-energy physics can be computationally expensive and time-consuming. To provide the most realistic data description at the ALICE experiment using Monte Carlo simulations, we investigated alternative solutions to generate the products of electromagnetic interactions initiated by slow neutrons in the Time Projection Chamber (TPC). Specifically, electrons and positrons from these processes can spiral repeatedly within the magnetic field in the detector (so-called "loopers") and tracking them via full transport frameworks, such as GEANT4, requires extensive computing resources. A fast simulation framework based on a Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network (WGAN) was developed to speed up the simulation process. Our solution is designed to bypass the significant computational cost associated with transporting neutrons to low energies by directly generating the kinematic properties (e.g., momentum, production vertex) of the resulting electron-positron pairs (from neutron capture) and electrons from Compton scattering. The generative model successfully reproduces the complex multidimensional distributions of the background tracks with high fidelity, maintaining agreement with the full GEANT4 simulation. By replacing the full transport of these specific background components with the WGAN inference, a tenfold speedup is achieved, making the simulation of loopers a negligible component of the total processing time. Moreover, the fast simulation framework is modular, allowing for the future inclusion of other background sources, such as nuclear spallation products.

</details>

The ratio is presented as a function of transverse momentum, $p_\rm{T}$, and rapidity, $y$, in the ranges $0.15 < p_\rm{T} < 2.5\rm{GeV}/c$ and $2.0 < y < 4.5$. The results are in good agreement with previous measurements at different centre-of-mass energies. They are compared to predictions from currently available event generators and can be used as input in future event generator tunings.

Method: 设计ANUBIS：在ATLAS洞穴顶部与竖井底部部署约1600 m² RPC构成双层径迹探测器（层间距~1 m），以洞穴上方空气为无屏蔽衰变体积；深度整合ATLAS触发与事件重建，实现背景压制；已通过proANUBIS原理验证装置（2024–2025运行）完成技术可行性验证。

Abstract: Long-lived particles (LLPs) arise naturally in many extensions of the Standard Model, notably in models with dark matter candidates or motivated by baryogenesis. LLPs produced at or above the electroweak scale with decay lengths of tens of metres escape the general-purpose LHC detectors and cannot be produced at sufficient rates in forward and beam-dump experiments. This Letter of Intent proposes the ANUBIS detector, which would instrument the ceiling of the ATLAS cavern and the bottom of its access shafts with about 1600~m$^2$ of resistive plate chambers in two tracking layers about 1~m apart, using the air-filled volume above the ATLAS detector as decay volume. The ANUBIS detector exploits the full ATLAS event information to suppress backgrounds, and exchanges triggers with the ATLAS experiment. With 3~ab$^{-1}$ at the High-Luminosity LHC, it would improve the sensitivity to exotic Higgs-boson decays into long-lived scalars by two to three orders of magnitude, probe heavy neutral leptons of all three flavours down to mixings of about $2\times10^{-8}$, and extend the reach for axion-like particles. The \textsl{pro}ANUBIS demonstrator, operated in the ATLAS cavern in 2024 and 2025, has validated the detector technology. The detector design, trigger concept, integration with the ATLAS experiment and a timeline towards installation in LHC Long Shutdown 4 are presented.

</details>

Abstract: In this pedagogical article we discuss the Dirac equation in arbitrary coordinate systems. We explain the need for connections when dealing with spinor fields and present the practical steps needed to compute the Dirac operator in a general setting, allowing for arbitrary coordinate systems and position-dependent orthonormal gamma matrices, both of which can simplify computations in certain scenarios. The extension to include minimally coupled gravity is straightforward. Examples are provided, including constructing the Dirac equation in spherical and cylindrical symmetry (with or without gravity) and the Kerr spacetime. We also briefly comment on the advantages and disadvantages of various tetrads and orthonormal gamma matrices. This manuscript should be accessible to readers who have studied the Dirac equation at an introductory level (e.g., in Cartesian coordinates in flat spacetime with constant gamma matrices). The emphasis is on the use of orthonormal frames, since these correspond to the coordinate systems most familiar to readers when dealing with most field theories, and represent the physical frames where measurements are performed. The aim is to get the reader quickly to the point of being able to do calculations, so the presentation favors a concise, pragmatic approach over extended derivations.

Abstract: We outline the history of the research of dynamic systems including higher derivatives in the Lagrangian, The story starts in 1744 when Euler published a book where the Euler--Lagrange differential equations for a HD system were first written. The next important landmarks were the papers of Ostrogradsky (1850) and of Pais and Uhlenbeck (1950). Many articles written in the second half of the last century were influenced by a misconception that was widespread at the time --- it was believed that higher derivatives necessarily bring about a violation of unitarity. We understand today that this is not the case and there are many systems with benign ghosts where HD are present but unitarity is not violated. In this review, we present and discuss several examples of such (quantum) mechanical systems and 2D field theories. Then we will briefly discuss higher-derivative gravity theories in 4 dimensions. In that case, the question whether the ghosts there are benign or not has a special status due to the absence of a nonperturbative Hamiltonian and does not have a definite answer yet. However, if one restricts oneself to classical theory, one can argue that the ghosts {\it are} benign (benign classical ghostly system is a system that needs an infinite time to reach a singularity, and it seems to the case both in ordinary and in HD gravity: the cosmic censorship conjecture implies that the singularities are always surrounded by horizons, and it takes an infinite time on the clocks of a distant observer to reach a horizon). Finally, I discuss supersymmetric HD theories: conformal supergravity, where the ghosts are also probably benign, and then an interesting supersymmetric HD gauge theory in 6 dimensions. It involves a dimensionless coupling constant, is renormalizable and rather beautiful. Unfortunately, it is not unitary --- ghosts are not benign there.

</details>

Abstract: We study the kink and particle spectra for the massive $\phi_{2,1}$-deformations of minimal models $\mathcal M_{k+1,k+2}$ via truncated conformal space approach (TCSA) on a defect circle. These non-invertible symmetries are spontaneously broken, and thus label the vacua and organize the kinks and particles into their multiplets in terms of quiver diagrams. We employ a finite-volume truncated Hamiltonian approach on defect Hilbert spaces, $\mathcal H_{X_i}$ for $X_i\in\mathcal C_k$, to study these quivers in the IR theories. The symmetry defect can then close an individual kink on a circle. We first establish the relation between states of definite fractional momentum on the defect circle and physical kink and particle states labelled by their vacuum pairs. Using form factors of local fields, we independently measure the squared amplitudes between the states of the two bases in defect TCSA. Combining these measurements with defect TCSA spectra, we reconstruct the quiver diagrams for $k=3,4,5$ without assuming them in advance. In particular, for $k=4$ we reconstruct the distinct quivers of the ordered and disordered phases. For $k=5$, we identify the quivers of both the light and heavy multiplets.

Abstract: Energy correlators are infrared safe because each detector weights a parton by its energy, but a weight in the arrival time makes them infrared unsafe in gauge theories. Abstract: We construct the black hole infrared triangle of scalar QED on a stretched Schwarzschild horizon at tree level. In the covariant phase space of the near-horizon Maxwell field, the soft charge is a corner term. The memory is the zero mode of the gauge-invariant radiative field along the generators. It is conjugate to the boundary gauge mode that labels the degenerate horizon vacua, and Gauss' law fixes it by the flux of charge through the horizon. The Noether flux of the residual gauge transformation through the stretched horizon is not conserved by the scattering, because charged matter crosses the stretched horizon in both directions. The flux with its matter part removed is the soft charge, which generates the large gauge transformation. Its Ward identity is equivalent to a soft photon theorem in every radiative partial wave, which we derive from the near-horizon Feynman rules and, where these do not control the emission, from the field equations at leading order. The main difference from null infinity is that on a stretched horizon both radial branches of the field are independent boundary data. The hard charge is a radial flux. With both branches present it weights each leg by the direction of its radial motion, and the soft factor is a sign per leg. The endpoint data on the two branches give two boundary observables, the time-integrated transverse magnetic and electric fields. The field radiated by a scattering process contributes only to the first, where it cancels the change in the Coulomb memory of the charges, so that the soft charge is conserved. A charge that crosses the horizon enters both. We also discuss the infrared divergence, the dressing of the external states, and the fate of the memory in the Hartle--Hawking state, where it performs a random walk driven by the Hawking radiation.

Abstract: The surface impedance of a body is fixed by its interior alone and encodes its entire tidal response. We derive bounds on Love numbers and quasinormal modes from constraints on the impedance that follow from either of two properties of a stable body: it is lossless, meaning that it dissipates no energy, or it is passive, meaning that it never gives back more energy than it has received. For a lossless body, every dynamical Love number is bounded from above and below, exactly in general relativity, in any renormalization scheme and at any scale. Earlier bounds are exact for Newtonian stars but fail for compact stars, because they take the response to arbitrarily fast tides to be zero or non-negative, whereas in general relativity it is negative. The quasinormal modes of a lossless body are also excluded from a region of the complex frequency plane set by the mass and radius. Each known Love number enlarges the excluded region. Conversely, the quasinormal modes bound each Love number from below, given the lower ones. For a passive body of horizon scale, such as a black hole, the static Love number lies in a window that closes onto the vanishing black-hole value as the body's surface approaches the horizon.

</details>

All known supersymmetric AdS$_4$ vacua of this theory lie in an $\mathcal N=1$ truncation containing seven chiral multiplets. In this paper, we work out the uplift of this seven-chiral sector, providing a unified eleven-dimensional geometric description of all known supersymmetric AdS$_4$ solutions in this class. We then specialise to the $\mathcal N=1$ vacua with U(1)$^2$ and SO(3) symmetry, obtain their new $D=11$ uplifts and bring out their internal geometries. One U(1) acts on the fibre, while the other generates the only continuous isometry of the deformed $\mathbb{CP}^3$ base. This spacetime provides a concrete bottom-up arena to explore the imprints of rotation on probes in non-relativistic holography. First, we compute the holographic static quark-antiquark potential following the standard prescription that relates this boundary observable to the on shell-action of a string configuration whose endpoints are anchored at the boundary. We find that the screening length decreases both when the angular momentum is increased at fixed temperature and when the temperature is increased at fixed angular momentum. The former reflects centrifugal effects on the $q\bar{q}$ pair, whereas the latter stems from enhanced thermal fluctuations, which weaken correlations at a given separation. Second, we compute the growth rate of the Krylov complexity, using the holographic prescription that relates this observable to the momentum of a massive particle probe falling into the IR. Abstract: We investigate how Lorentzian non-commutative smearing affects entanglement and horizon brightened acceleration radiation (HBAR) outside a black hole. For a massless scalar field, tracing over the interior partner gives the exterior quantum channel, its logarithmic negativity, mutual information, and Bell entanglement fidelity. At fixed mass and Killing frequency, increasing the smearing lowers the surface gravity, increases the accessible correlations, and suppresses the leading atomic emission probability. The horizon displacement begins at order $\sqrt{\Theta}$, while the thermal correction at infinity begins at order $\Theta$. At fixed local frequency, the redshift introduces a term of order $\sqrt{\Theta}$ at fixed areal radius. Holding the proper distance fixed, it cancels this term and recovers the common Rindler response close to the horizon. For a freely falling atom, emission and absorption obey detailed balance at the Hawking temperature in the leading horizon approximation. Random atomic injection drives the selected mode from the vacuum to a thermal state with an exact relaxation law and nonnegative entropy production. Near equilibrium, we obtain $\dot S_{\rm HBAR}\simeq\dot A_p/4$ when the radiated energy is drawn from horizon heat, where $\dot A_p$ is the positive radiative area loss. When the radiation is assigned to ADM mass loss at fixed smearing length $\ell=4\sqrt{\Theta/\pi}$, the result is $\dot S_{\rm HBAR}\simeq\dot A_p/[4(1-\ell/r_+)]$. The mass dependence of the exterior source thus enters the entropy per unit area lost at order $\sqrt{\Theta}$.

</details>


### [98] [A Pole-Cut Formula for Holographic Extremal Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34824)

Method: 基于全息对偶，从有限温度QNM乘积公式出发取零温极限；结合复频域解析延拓、边界源函数零点分析、分支割线相位跳变提取、OPE约束分析及数值验证；利用光学距离刻画复时间奇点。

Result: 获得极值RN黑膜上标量谱函数的‘极点-割线’因式分解公式：ρ(ω) ∝ ∏_n (ω − ω_n) × exp[∫ dω′ Δφ(ω′)/(ω′ − ω)]；发现QNM渐近分布与相位不连续函数渐近行为受OPE强约束；确认复时间奇点位置由边界至奇点的光学距离决定。

Abstract: We show that the holographic spectral function of a neutral massless scalar on the extremal Reissner-Nordstrom black brane in $AdS_5$ admits an elegant formula in terms of the quasi-normal modes (QNMs) and branch-cut data. In the extremal case, the spectral data comprises the zeros of the boundary source function, i.e. the QNMs, as well as its phase discontinuity across the branch cut on the negative imaginary axis of the complex frequency plane. This formula neatly factorizes into an infinite product over the QNM poles and a factor encoding the branch cut discontinuity. We derive this pole-cut formula as the zero-temperature limit of the QNM product formula for holographic thermal correlators, with the branch-cut contribution arising from the coalescence of the QNMs on the negative imaginary axis. We also clarify the role of the near-horizon $AdS_2$ region in this context. Consistency with the OPE of the boundary correlator provides extremal sum-rules involving both the QNMs as well as the phase discontinuity function. We also find that this holographic consistency forces the asymptotic form of the QNMs to be related to the asymptotics of the phase discontinuity function. We verify our formula and these OPE constraints numerically. We also demonstrate that, in the time domain, the pole and cut factors each produce complex-time singularities, whose location is determined by the optical distance between the boundary and the black-brane singularity. We expect the pole-cut factorization structure of spectral functions to hold for more general fluctuations of extremal black branes.

</details>

Method: 基于带电尘埃薄壳模型，构造以外部Killing时间为演化参数的哈密顿量（即ADM能量），通过正则变换揭示其在临界区域与负势Liouville理论的等价性，并实施正则量子化以计算反弹概率分布。

Abstract: It has been emphasized recently that some extremal black holes are "critical" solutions, lying at the threshold between gravitational collapse and dispersion. We study the effect of quantum gravity on critical solutions for the collapse of a thin shell of charged dust. We consider the Hamiltonian that generates the shell equation of motion with respect to the exterior Killing time and coincides with the ADM energy on-shell. Near criticality, this Hamiltonian is shown to be canonically equivalent to that of a Liouville theory with negative potential. After quantization, this model predicts that a collapsing shell has a non-trivial probability of undergoing a quantum mechanical bounce when its energy above extremality is comparable to the "breakdown scale". The bounce probability approaches unity at extremality. Hence this model predicts that the critical surface in the classical theory is thickened quantum mechanically into a "critical layer". The probability density function for the black hole energy is suppressed below the breakdown scale in the same way as found recently by Turiaci and Wu.

</details>

Conclusion: 线缺陷OPE是描述相关形变下延展缺陷融合的有效工具，兼具微扰可计算性与非微扰可检验性，为缺陷物理与非局域算符代数提供了新范式。

Abstract: We discuss the fusion of parallel line defects in theories defined by relevant deformations of a conformal fixed point. We propose an operator product expansion to describe this limit in terms of a Wilsonian effective action whose coefficients are computable in conformal perturbation theory around the UV. This allows one to fix, order by order, the fusion limit of correlation functions involving the defects, up to non-perturbative coefficients associated to correlation functions (condensates) on the fused defect. It thus generalizes the familiar OPE of local operators in QFT. As a non-perturbative test of our proposal, we consider the short-distance potential between two magnetic impurities in the $2d$ Ising model. We find excellent agreement between the OPE framework and DMRG extractions of the potential and condensates (the latter also computed exactly with integrability). In addition, we consider a number of solvable models to illustrate different features of the framework.

</details>

Abstract: Type-IIB string compactifications typically give rise to a significant number of RR-photons. These are generically massless and do not possess any light charged states, making them `superhidden'. However, since supersymmetry is present at least at some high scale, corresponding `dark photinos' are also predicted. The kinetic mixing of the photons together with the SUSY breaking mass terms of the photinos makes the latter observable. We identify geometries in which the mixing is induced by Wilson lines and show that their expectation values are generically non-zero. As a result, most of the dark photinos, which are lighter than the visible sector SUSY breaking scale, mix significantly with the bino and hence couple to the Standard Model sufficiently strongly to leave potentially observable traces. In particular, volume modulus and inflaton decays generically induce a cosmological abundance of dark photinos that can explain dark matter. We find that the `natural Photinoverse dark matter parameter space' is already accessible to current experiments, notably gamma ray observations, which therefore have discovery potential in the near future.

</details>

Abstract: We determine the global form of the gauge groups of the orthogonal and symplectic type 0 orientifolds, using the constraints from perturbative and brane states. *Dmitry S. Ageev,Yulia A. Ageeva,Anna S. Bernakevich*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立神经网络场论（NN-FT）与全息对偶（特别是AdS/CFT）之间的严格联系，阐明神经架构如何编码体时空动力学与边界共形可观测量。

Method: 构造傅里叶-贝塞尔余弦神经元，使其协方差精确复现有限宽度下的正则化大质量AdS传播子；通过参数测度变形或辅助二值神经元统计两种方式引入局域四次相互作用；直接应用BDHM字典将神经系综映射至边界关联函数。

Result: 1) 精确实现大质量标量AdS传播子（有限宽度）与高斯场论（无限宽度极限）；2) 导出体-边传播子、共形两点函数及主导四点接触Witten图；3) 发现不同神经架构可生成相同主导边界关联函数但定义不等价体理论。

Conclusion: NN-FT为研究相互作用标量全息提供了严格框架，神经架构的选择决定了体理论的等价类，而非仅边界观测；该工作统一了深度学习表征与AdS/CFT中的几何/动力学自由度。

Abstract: We develop a neural realization of massive scalar field theory on Euclidean anti--de Sitter space and its holographic boundary observables within neural network field theory (NN--FT). We construct Fourier--Bessel cosine neurons whose covariance reproduces the regulated massive AdS propagator exactly at finite network width, with the Gaussian field theory recovered in the infinite-width limit. We introduce quartic interactions through two complementary approaches. The first implements a prescribed interaction by deforming the neural parameter measure, while the second generates a local quartic interaction from the statistics of auxiliary binary neurons. Applying the BDHM prescription directly to the neural ensemble, we recover the scalar bulk-to-boundary propagator, the conformal boundary two-point function, and the leading connected four-point contact Witten diagram. We further show that distinct neural architectures can reproduce the same leading boundary correlators while defining inequivalent bulk theories. These results establish a framework for studying interacting scalar holography within NN--FT and clarify the role of neural architecture in encoding bulk dynamics and boundary observables.

</details>


### [104] [Energy plateau cascades from complex-time local quenches in thermal two-dimensional CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33283)
*Dmitry S. Ageev,Vladimir A. Bykov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维热态共形场论中局域算符猝变在复时间演化下的行为，揭示了不同共形族竞争导致的阶梯状能量平台结构，并用共形Ward恒等式在柱面与环面上计算局域能量密度；提出了低温谱描述，证明对固定紧致幺正CFT，仅有限个主通道主导平台序列；最后探讨了大中心荷极限下轻算符平台与热AdS中少粒子态的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子系统中条件动力学的有效描述，特别是热态初始条件下局域扰动引发的非平衡能量输运和弛豫行为。

Method: 运用复时间演化框架、共形Ward恒等式（在柱面和环面几何上）、主态谱展开、低温极限分析以及大中心荷渐近方法。

Result: 发现热态猝变可产生近似均匀的能量平台级联，各平台趋近于不同主态能量；平台高度由主导主通道决定，跃迁由共形维数和算符矩阵元调控；对固定紧致幺正CFT，仅有限个主通道贡献于主平台序列；大中心荷下轻算符平台对应热AdS中少粒子激发。

Conclusion: 热态局域猝变展现出普适的阶梯式能量弛豫结构，其动力学由共形对称性严格约束，且在不同极限下与全息对偶及谱理论建立深刻联系。

Abstract: In this work, we study local operator quenches in thermal two-dimensional conformal field theories with complex-time evolution, which can be viewed as an effective description of conditional dynamics in a system interacting with an environment. In contrast to quenches of the vacuum, the thermal initial state allows several conformal families to participate in the evolution. We show that their competition can produce cascades of approximately homogeneous energy plateaux, with successive plateaux approaching the energies of different primary states. Using conformal Ward identities on the cylinder and torus, we determine the local energy density and illustrate this behavior in free-field theories, the Ising model and Wess--Zumino--Witten models. We develop a cold-limit spectral description in which the dominant primary channel determines the height of each plateau, while conformal dimensions and operator matrix elements govern the transitions between them. For a fixed compact unitary CFT, we show that only finitely many primary channels can contribute to the leading plateau sequence. Finally, we discuss the large-central-charge regime, relating light-operator plateaux to few-particle sectors in thermal AdS and examining the limitations of this picture for heavy quenches.

</details>


### [105] [RMT${}_2$: Random Spectral Correlations for CFT${}_2$ and AdS${}_3$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33291)
*Jan Boruch,Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Felix M. Haehl,Eric Perlmutter,Moshe Rozali*

Main category: hep-th

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个既满足随机矩阵统计又严格保持SL(2,ℤ)模不变性的量子引力微观模型框架，以刻画AdS₃中多边界虫洞的普适低能行为，并解决传统RMT在含自旋谱和模对称性之间的张力。

Method: 将随机矩阵关联函数经梅林变换后，在SL(2,ℤ)下作Poincaré求和，引入自旋量子数以分块实现各主算子扇区的极值随机性；将结果投影至Eisenstein系列与Maass尖点形式构成的模形式正交基；结合Airy模型的亏格展开与模不变重求和技术，导出谱形因子的ramp-plateau结构。

Result: 建立了RMT₂完整框架：给出任意自旋主算子扇区中模不变两点及三点关联函数的显式构造（含单位正交两类对称性）；证明其具有模不变拓扑展开；谱形因子在重求和后仍保留ramp-plateau特征；首次给出AdS₃中三边界虫洞的普适RMT预言表达式。

Conclusion: RMT₂是首个严格融合随机矩阵统计、自旋结构与SL(2,ℤ)模不变性的微观框架，为二维共形场论的量子混沌、模不变谱统计及AdS₃量子引力中的多边界虫洞提供了统一数学基础与可观测量基准。

Abstract: We present the full framework of RMT${}_2$, a consistent unification of random matrix theory and modular invariance. The systematic prescription, which augments the existing construction by incorporating spin, lifts random matrix correlators to $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ Poincaré sums of their Mellin transforms, in a manner which enforces random matrix correlations near extremality in every spinning primary sector. The resulting RMT${}_2$ correlators are further expressible in terms of an orthogonal basis of Eisenstein series and Maass cusp forms. We demonstrate the construction by lifting the two-point correlators at arbitrary genus of the Airy model, in both unitary and orthogonal symmetry classes, exhibiting the modular-invariant topological expansion of RMT${}_2$; a resummation of the genus expansion shows that the modular-invariant spectral form factor preserves the ramp-plateau structure. Holographically, RMT${}_2$ provides a concrete benchmark and explicit candidate expressions for comparison with off-shell wormhole amplitudes in AdS${}_3$ gravity, with any number of torus boundaries. We explicitly compute the universal RMT${}_2$ three-point correlator, corresponding to the putative three-boundary wormhole.

</details>


### [106] [On the Structure Constants of ${\cal N}=2$ Liouville Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33343)
*Jingxin Tu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了N=2时空类利奥维尔理论的三点半结构常数，通过费米型FZZ对偶建立其与SL(2,R)/U(1)超余弦理论的代数对应，并匹配零模；中性与带电扇区结构常数分别对应SL(2,R) WZW模型的谱流守恒与非守恒三点半函数，并确定其相对归一化并数值验证交叉对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N=2时空类利奥维尔理论缺乏b↔b⁻¹自对偶性，标准bootstrap方法失效；受费米型FZZ对偶启发，需建立其与SL(2,R)/U(1)超余弦理论的精确代数映射以求解结构常数。

Method: 利用N=2 Liouville与SL(2,R)/U(1)超余弦理论间的费米型FZZ对偶，构造算符映射与零模匹配；在超空间中推导三点半函数，将中性/带电扇区结构常数分别关联至SL(2,R) WZW模型的谱流守恒/非守恒三点半函数；通过共形不变性与超对称约束确定扇区间相对归一化。

Result: 得到N=2时空类利奥维尔理论完整的三点半结构常数表达式：中性扇区对应SL(2,R) WZW模型谱流守恒结构常数，两个带电扇区对应谱流违反结构常数；给出扇区间精确相对归一化因子；数值验证了四点函数的交叉对称性。

Conclusion: N=2时空类利奥维尔理论的结构常数可通过FZZ对偶完全由SL(2,R) WZW模型的已知数据重构，证实其作为非紧致N=2超共形场论的完备可解性，并为AdS₃/CFT₂对应中NS-NS背景下的D-brane物理提供关键输入。

Abstract: We show the structure constants of $\mathcal{N}=2$ spacelike Liouville theory. The absence of self-duality under $b\leftrightarrow b^{-1}$, where $b$ is the Liouville coupling, prevents a straightforward application of the standard bootstrap methods used in $\mathcal{N}=0$ and $\mathcal{N}=1$ Liouville theory. Motivated by the fermionic FZZ duality with the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})/U(1)$ supercoset theory, we establish an algebraic correspondence between the two theories, including a matching of their null vectors. We then derive the three-point structure constants in superspace. The neutral sector structure constants are associated with the spectral-flow-preserving structure constant of the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ WZW model, while the two charged sectors correspond to its spectral-flow-violating structure constants. Finally, we give the correct relative normalization between the neutral and charged sectors and test crossing symmetry numerically.

</details>


### [107] [Identifying Renormalization Group Interfaces of Massless WZW Flows $\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_k \to \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33354)
*Yuma Furuta,Yuya Kusuki,Toshiki Onagi,Yin Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文识别了质量为零的 Wess-Zumino-Witten 模型中 su(2)ₖ → su(2)₁ 流在所有奇数 k ≥ 3 下的重整化群界面（RG interface），其结构由对角流代数 su(2)ₖ₊₁ 和 ℤ₂ 中心对称性唯一确定；该结果预言了不同 UV 流向同一 IR 固定点时界面激发谱的可区分模式，并被自旋链数值计算（k = 3, 5）所验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临界流（critical flows）中紫外（UV）与红外（IR）理论之间算符映射的精确结构，特别是通过 RG interface 描述短距与长距物理的联系；针对长期猜想的 su(2)ₖ → su(2)₁ WZW 流，需明确其拓扑界面的具体代数实现。

Method: 利用全局 SU(2) 对称性约束界面必须携带对角电流代数 ŝu(2)ₖ₊₁；结合 ℤ₂ 中心对称性选择其在 su(2)ₖ₊₁ 表示中的具体最高权（即界面标记）；辅以自旋链模型的数值对角化提取低能界面激发谱。

Result: 对每个奇数 k ≥ 3，su(2)ₖ → su(2)₁ RG interface 在拓扑等价类中被唯一确定为 su(2)ₖ₊₁ 的特定不可约表示（由 ℤ₂ 选择），且其低能激发谱依赖于 k，即使 IR 理论相同（均为 su(2)₁）。

Conclusion: RG interface 不仅编码共形流的普适性，还携带 UV 数据（如水平 k）的非平凡痕迹；该工作为 WZW 模型间临界流提供了首个严格构造的、可检验的界面描述，并确立了中心对称性在界面分类中的关键作用。

Abstract: A renormalization-group interface encodes how a flow relates operators at short and long distances. We identify this interface up to topological dressing for the conjectured massless $\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_k \to \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten flows at every odd level $k\geq 3$. Global $SU(2)$ symmetry requires the interface to preserve a diagonal current algebra at level $k+1$, built from the currents on its two sides. The $\mathbb{Z}_2$ center symmetry then selects which representation labels the interface. This identification predicts distinct patterns of low-lying interface excitations for flows to the same infrared fixed point. Spin-chain calculations reproduce these patterns for $k=3$ and $5$, providing independent evidence for both conjectured flows.

</details>


### [108] [An Argument for the Weak Gravity Conjecture from Constrained Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33415)
*Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Luca V. Iliesiu,Dawid Maskalaniec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究引力路径积分（GPI）在渐近平直时空Einstein-Maxwell理论中的正定性约束，发现若无粒子满足弱引力猜想（WGC），多边界虫洞振幅将违反GPI正定性；而若存在满足WGC的荷电粒子，则虫洞失稳，从而恢复正定性；该论证进一步要求磁WGC成立，且对多个U(1)规范场的情形需满足凸包条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保引力路径积分定义的希尔伯特空间内积具有正半定性，这是低能有效理论具备一致量子诠释的前提，但该正定性在有效理论中并不显见，需由紫外完备性（如WGC）来保障。

Method: 构造一类新的受约束瞬子以计算多边界虫洞振幅，并结合正定性条件与WGC进行反证分析：假设WGC不成立则导出GPI非正定；反之，若WGC成立，则虫洞出现失稳模，使振幅可重整化并恢复正定性。

Result: 证明了在D≥4渐近平直Einstein-Maxwell-物质理论中，GPI正定性等价于弱引力猜想（含磁WGC）与凸包条件的联合成立；多边界虫洞振幅的正定性严格依赖于最轻荷电粒子是否满足WGC。

Conclusion: WGC不仅是紫外完备性的经验判据，更是低能有效理论中GPI正定性（即希尔伯特空间结构一致性）的必要条件；磁WGC与凸包条件是多U(1)情形下正定性得以维持的不可约要求。

Abstract: The gravitational path integral (GPI) computes inner products between states of open and closed universes in any low-energy effective theory. A well-defined Hilbert space requires these inner products to be positive semidefinite, but this positivity is not manifest in the effective theory. In this paper, we study the constraints that positivity imposes on Einstein--Maxwell theory coupled to charged matter in asymptotically flat space in $D\geq 4$ dimensions. We start by constructing a new class of constrained instantons that compute multi-boundary wormhole amplitudes. We show that, under mild assumptions, if no charged particle satisfies the weak gravity conjecture (WGC), these amplitudes violate positivity of the GPI. If instead some particle satisfies the WGC, the wormholes develop an instability that allows positivity to be restored. The same argument shows that positivity can only be restored if the magnetic WGC holds and the convex hull condition applies to theories with several $\mathrm{U}(1)$ gauge fields.

</details>


### [109] [Probe branes from equivariant volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33445)
*Kiril Hristov,Pedro Vicente Marto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，用于计算全息对偶中M2和D3膜在BPS背景下的经典欧几里得作用量，通过相对等变配对与等变体积实现背景度规无关的正则化，并在多个AdS/CFT实例中验证了其对瞬子指数、Wilson圈、巨引力子及面缺陷等非微扰可观测量的精确再现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统刻画全息对偶中非微扰效应（如巨引力子、表面缺陷、瞬子贡献）的经典膜作用量，需摆脱对具体背景度规的依赖，建立普适、几何化且可正则化的计算框架。

Method: 发展基于相对等变上同调的几何方法：将BPS膜的世界体作用量表达为内部/时空流形上的相对等变配对；在满足延拓与边界条件下，用等变体积替代显式度规积分以实现无度规正则化。

Result: 成功导出M2膜在任意BPS背景（含AdS₄×SE₇）与D3膜在IIB五形式扇区（含AdS₅×SE₅）中的作用量公式；复现并改进ABJM、ADHM、N=4 SYM等对偶中的瞬子指数、有限Wilson圈、M2/D3巨引力子及表面缺陷作用量；给出黑洞探针在Klebanov-Witten背景中的作用量测试。

Conclusion: 经典欧几里得膜作用量本质上由背景超对称结构与世界体拓扑共同决定，可通过等变几何完全编码；该框架统一处理紧致与非紧致、内空间与时空方向的超对称膜，为全息非微扰物理提供了普适的几何语言。

Abstract: Classical Euclidean probe actions control non-perturbative weights and extended observables in holography. We present a geometric prescription for M2-branes in arbitrary BPS backgrounds and D3-branes in the five-form sector of type IIB. The framework accommodates compact and non-compact supersymmetric worldvolumes in both internal and spacetime directions, expressing their actions as relative equivariant pairings. Under stated extension and boundary assumptions, equivariant volumes evaluate these pairings without explicit background metrics and provide a regularization for non-compact probes. Examples in $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times SE_7$ backgrounds dual to ABJM, ADHM and flavored quiver theories reproduce and refine worldsheet-type instanton exponents and finite Wilson-loop actions. We also recover M2 giant-graviton and surface-defect actions in $\mathrm{AdS}_7\times S^4$. D3 probes in $SE_5$ compactifications dual to $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills and Klebanov--Witten theory provide tests involving giant gravitons, black hole probes and surface defects.

</details>


### [110] [Late-time OTOCs in Toda conformal field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33744)
*Giwan Kim,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维A_{N-1} Toda CFT中半退化原初算符的晚时间OTOC行为，发现N=2（Liouville）时OTOC按t^{-3/2}衰减而非常数；N=3时通过横向围道夹逼将谱积分降维，连续相中正则化边缘表现为离散态，OTOC趋于纯编织相，且该边缘块等价于电荷中性Weyl框架下的自由场关联函数；N≥4时一维约化违反交叉对称性所要求的最低中间权的界，故N=2,3相图无法直接推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高阶Toda理论中混沌动力学的普适性与边界，特别是OTOC的晚时间行为如何依赖于李代数秩N及谱结构（连续/离散），并检验交叉对称性对OTOC渐近的约束。

Method: 基于共形自举与围道分析，对半退化原初算符构造OTOC的谱表示；利用横向围道夹逼实现N=3情形下谱积分的维度约化；结合Weyl反射对称性与电荷中性条件识别边缘块的自由场实现；数值验证边缘块解析结构并检验交叉对称性约束。

Result: N=2：OTOC晚时间衰减为t^{-3/2}（修正先前常数极限）；N=3：连续相中OTOC趋近纯braiding相，边缘块为电荷中性Weyl帧下自由场关联函数；N≥4：一维约化违反交叉界，表明需引入低于约化连续边缘的贡献或非正定对角展开项。

Conclusion: Toda CFT中OTOC的晚时间行为强烈依赖于李代数秩N：仅N=2,3存在由边缘连续谱主导的可解相，而N≥4必须包含额外低权态或非标准谱结构以满足交叉对称性，揭示了高秩Toda理论中量子混沌与共形对称性之间的深层张力。

Abstract: We study late-time out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) of semi-degenerate primary fields in two-dimensional $A_{N-1}$ Toda conformal field theory. In Liouville theory ($N=2$), we find that the OTOC decays as $t^{-3/2}$ when the continuum edge dominates, correcting a previously reported constant limit. For $N=3$, a transverse contour pinch reduces the spectral integral to one dimension. Assuming that the reduced expansion is complete, in the continuous phase the regulated edge behaves as a discrete state, and the OTOC approaches a pure braiding phase. As we check numerically, the edge block is a free-field correlator in a charge-neutral Weyl frame; this explains the pure phase and is consistent with crossing symmetry. For both $N=2$ and $N=3$, a dominant discrete residue gives a constant late-time limit. For $N\geq4$, the same one-dimensional reduction violates a crossing bound on the lowest intermediate weight of a positive diagonal expansion. Hence the $N=2,3$ phase diagram does not extend to higher rank without additional contributions below the reduced continuum edge or outside a positive diagonal expansion.

</details>


### [111] [Defect Correlators from Supergravity: Finding the Missing Contact Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34082)
*Qi Chen,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了在存在缺陷（defects）时利用超引力计算全息关联函数所遇到的一个谜题：此前直接的超引力计算结果与bootstrap结果在缺陷两点函数上存在由接触项（contact contributions）导致的差异；作者指出该差异源于两个效应——体时空中的非线性Kaluza-Klein场重定义诱导的额外二次耦合，以及混合体-缺陷顶点中的离壳项导致缺陷传播子坍缩；并在4d N=4 SYM中两个具体情形（1/2-BPS Wilson线与RP^4背景）中验证了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和超引力直接计算与bootstrap方法在含缺陷的全息关联函数（尤其是缺陷两点函数）上的不一致，理解接触项起源的物理机制。

Method: 系统分析体时空非线性Kaluza-Klein场重定义对局域源的二次耦合修正，以及混合体-缺陷作用量中离壳项对缺陷传播子的贡献；在AdS/CFT对应框架下，结合超对称背景（如1/2-BPS Wilson线、RP^4）进行显式微扰计算与顶点分析。

Result: 确认两类机制——（i）非线性KK重定义引入的额外二次耦合，（ii）离壳混合顶点导致的缺陷传播子坍缩——共同生成缺失的接触项，从而完全复现bootstrap结果；在两个N=4 SYM缺陷背景下完成自洽验证。

Conclusion: 超引力计算中必须严格处理非线性KK结构与离壳顶点效应，否则将遗漏关键接触项；该工作为含缺陷全息体系的高阶关联函数计算建立了系统性微扰框架，并强化了AdS/CFT在非平凡边界/缺陷构型下的自洽性。

Abstract: We resolve a puzzle in the computation of holographic correlators using supergravity when defects are present. Previous direct supergravity calculations of defect two-point functions were found to differ from bootstrap results by a set of contact contributions. We show that the discrepancy arises from two effects: nonlinear Kaluza-Klein field redefinitions in the bulk, which induce additional quadratic couplings when applied consistently to localized sources, and off-shell terms in mixed bulk--defect vertices, which can collapse internal defect propagators. We demonstrate how these two mechanisms resolve the puzzle in two concrete examples in 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM, where we consider two-point correlators in the presence of a $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson line and on $\mathbb{RP}^4$.

</details>


### [112] [Robin--Hadamard states for unstable field in half-Minkowski spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34369)
*Bruno S. Felipe,João P. M. Pitelli,Claudio Dappiaggi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维半闵可夫斯基时空下满足吸引性Robin边界条件的无质量实标量场的量子化问题，证明了经典谱完备性所需的不稳定模对恢复量子场正则对易关系（CCR）同样关键；通过指定不稳定模的Cauchy协方差，构造了一族与总能量守恒相容的正定拟自由态，但该族中不存在同时满足正定性与平稳性的态，故所有态均显式破缺时间平移对称性；并给出了稳定真空部分两定点函数的位置空间闭式表达式，包含由CCR固定的不稳定部分贡献，其奇异性结构（含Robin依赖的对数项）符合Robin–Hadamard框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决半闵可夫斯基时空下Robin边界条件下无质量标量场量子化的基础问题，特别是不稳定模在量子化中的作用及其对态选择、对称性破缺和奇异性结构的影响。

Method: 采用谱分析与拟自由态构造方法，结合Cauchy数据协方差形式化处理不稳定模，利用Robin–Hadamard单点减除框架分析两点函数的奇异性结构。

Result: 1) 不稳定模是恢复CCR的必要条件；2) 构造出一族能量守恒的正定拟自由态，但无一为平稳态（即必破缺时间平移对称性）；3) 获得稳定真空两定点函数的闭式位置空间表达式，含直接与反射奇异性及Robin依赖的对数发散项；4) 所有构造态具有相同奇异性结构，满足Robin–Hadamard条件。

Conclusion: Robin边界下的不稳定模不仅是经典谱完备性的要求，更是量子正则结构的内在组成部分；其存在强制量子态破缺背景时间平移对称性，且不改变两点函数的共形奇异性类型，仅修正其Robin依赖的有限部分。

Abstract: We investigate the quantization of a real massless scalar field in four-dimensional half-Minkowski spacetime subject to an attractive Robin boundary condition. We show that the unstable modes, required for classical spectral completeness, are also essential to recover the canonical commutation relations (CCR) of the complete quantum field. By specifying the Cauchy covariances of the unstable sector, we construct a family of positive quasifree states compatible with the conservation of total energy. Within this family, positivity is incompatible with stationarity, so that the resulting states necessarily break the time-translation symmetry of the background. We also obtain a closed position-space representation of the two-point function for the chosen stable vacuum sector, including the contribution fixed by the CCR in the unstable sector. The resulting expression exhibits the direct and reflected singularities, including Robin-dependent logarithmic terms. Since the remaining unstable contribution is smooth, all states in the constructed family share the same singularity structure, consistent with the Robin--Hadamard framework.

</details>


### [113] [Quark-Loop Corrections and Auxiliary Field-Strength Mixing in First-Order QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34421)
*Hasnat Mohammad Nayeem*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了杨-米尔斯理论一阶形式中胶子场强作为独立辅助场F时，夸克对这一结构的影响；发现夸克发散引入了f(A)·f(A)项，需通过辅助场F与场强f(A)的混合进行重整化；重夸克阈值效应导致有限混合系数，并在低能下将辅助场修正为包含轻夸克流的非局域表达式，从而分离长程胶子交换与短程重夸克效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在一阶形式Yang-Mills理论中，当胶子场强F被设为独立辅助场时，如何自洽地纳入夸克动力学及其对规范结构的量子修正，特别是处理夸克引发的不可忽略的发散项f(A)·f(A)，该类项无法通过单纯场重标度消除。

Method: 采用背景场方法（background-field method）与一般协变规范，结合圈图计算与维度正规化（1/\bar\epsilon极点），系统分析夸克圈对辅助场F、规范场A及耦合常数的紫外发散贡献；特别考察重夸克积分出后的阈值匹配与低能有效算符展开。

Result: 1) 夸克导致F与f(A)发生混合：F₀ = F + z f(A)，其中z = −αₛT_F N_f/(6π\bar\epsilon)；2) 重夸克积分出后产生有限阈值系数c = (αₛT_F/6π) ln(M²/μ²)，其β函数贡献精确给出重夸克对αₛ跑动的份额；3) 在M以下能标，保留领头幂次修正时，F = f(A) + (gαₛT_F/15πM²)(DₐJᵦ − DᵦJₐ)，即F不再是纯几何场强，而包含由轻夸克流J诱导的色-动量相关修正。

Conclusion: 在一阶形式Yang-Mills+QCD中，夸克不仅驱动规范耦合跑动，更根本性地改变辅助场F的物理含义：高能下F渐近趋于场强f(A)，低能下则承载轻夸克流的非局域响应；该结构自然实现αₛ的解耦，并将长程胶子传播与短程重夸克接触相互作用分离，为构建色单态有效理论提供严格场论基础。

Abstract: In the first-order formulation of Yang-Mills theory the gluon field strength is an independent auxiliary field $F$, and the only gluon self-interaction is a momentum-independent cubic vertex. We determine how quarks, which couple only to the gauge field, act on this structure. Their divergences are multiples of $f(A)\cdot f(A)$, a term absent from the first-order Lagrangian that no rescaling can produce. Working with the background-field method in a general covariant gauge, we find that the quark sector is renormalized entirely by the mixing of the auxiliary field with the field strength known from pure Yang-Mills theory, $F_0=F+z\,f(A)$, with quark coefficient $z=-\alpha_sT_FN_f/(6\pi\bar\epsilon)$, together with a rescaling of the gluon field and no rescaling of $F$, so that the renormalized auxiliary field runs into the gluon field strength. When a heavy quark of mass $M$ is integrated out, the same mixing survives as a finite threshold coefficient $c=(\alpha_sT_F/6\pi)\ln(M^2/\mu^2)$, whose scale dependence is exactly the heavy-quark share of the running, and the invariant combination of the couplings reproduces the decoupling of $\alpha_s$. Below the threshold, when the leading power correction is kept as a gauge-sector operator, the auxiliary field is no longer the field strength: $F=f(A)+(g\alpha_sT_F/15\pi M^2)(D_\alpha J_\beta-D_\beta J_\alpha)$, with $J$ the light-quark colour current. We verify this identity against the full theory and show that it separates the long-distance gluon exchange, carried by $F$, from the short-distance heavy-quark effect, carried by a four-quark contact term.

</details>


### [114] [Superconformal Index Identities for Dual Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34487)
*Min-xin Huang,Gao-fu Ren,Xin Wang*

TL;DR: 本文研究了三类四维超共形对偶理论的超共形指数恒等式：1) 利用Rains对角迹变换严格证明了SO(2n+1)与USp(2n) N=4 SYM的S-对偶Schur指数相等；2) 在UV赋值R(Q)=0下，严格证明了Kutasov-Schwimmer对偶在独立椭圆模p,q下的指数恒等式，并在p=q^k子流形上退化为普通SQCD恒等式；3) 对Cho-Song提出的Argyres-Douglas理论（H₁及四族高阶）的quiver/单群对偶，验证并部分证明了其 flavored index 与广义Coulomb index 的匹配，包括H₁全flavored指数的严格相等及各族广义Coulomb指数的乘积公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证强耦合下超对称规范理论的S-对偶、场论重正化群流及Argyres-Douglas超共形固定点的对偶性预言，通过超共形指数这一受保护可观测量提供非微扰证据。

Method: 运用Rains的对角迹变换（diagonal trace transformation）处理椭圆超几何积分；采用flavored Schur指数与广义Coulomb指数作为对偶检验工具；利用椭圆模参数的特殊取值（如p=q^k）实现不同对偶框架间的解析约化；对Argyres-Douglas理论采用quiver与单群描述的指数计算与比较。

Result: 1) SO(2n+1)/USp(2n) N=4 SYM的flavored Schur指数S-对偶恒等式被严格证明；2) Kutasov-Schwimmer对偶在任意p,q下的指数恒等式成立，且在p=q^k时还原为SQCD恒等式；3) H₁理论的全flavored指数在两种描述下完全相等；四族AD理论的广义Coulomb指数在两种描述下均给出Buican-Jiang猜想的乘积形式。

Conclusion: 三类重要超共形对偶——S-对偶、Kutasov-Schwimmer对偶与Argyres-Douglas理论的quiver/单群对偶——均在超共形指数层面获得严格或强有力的支持，证实了其非微扰一致性，并揭示了椭圆超几何结构在对偶性中的核心作用。

Abstract: We study superconformal index identities for three classes of dual four-dimensional gauge theories. For $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory with gauge groups $SO(2n+1)$ and $USp(2n)$, we prove at every rank the equality of flavored Schur indices predicted by $S$-duality, using Rains's diagonal trace transformation. For Kutasov--Schwimmer duality with adjoint superpotential $\textrm{Tr} X^{k+1}$, we prove the index identity between $SU(k+1)$ and $SU(k-1)$ with two flavors at independent elliptic nomes $p,q$, in the ultraviolet assignment $R(Q)=0$. On the locus $p=q^k$, we reduce the identity to that of ordinary supersymmetric QCD whenever the specialized expressions are generically regular. Finally, we test the dualities conjectured by Cho and Song between quiver and single-group descriptions of four families of Argyres--Douglas theories. For the rank-one $H_1$ theory we prove equality of the two full flavored indices, and for each family at arbitrary rank we evaluate the generalized Coulomb index in both descriptions, obtaining the product formula conjectured by Buican and Jiang.

</details>


### [115] [Power-law tunneling in the Razavy hyperbolic double well: Heun connection formulas and their resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34588)
*Jie Ren,Xuanzhuang Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究对称Razavy双曲双势阱中的非微扰能级劈裂，通过将其双合流Heun连接问题与精确WKB和准精确可解性关联，在弱耦合极限下利用Morse近似、反射分解、不规则块连接公式及椭圆积分方法，系统计算了能级劈裂的渐近展开（含Gamma函数系数、量子涨落修正与三遍穿越对数项），并在整数衰减指数情形下通过Floquet模约化处理奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强非谐势中非微扰量子隧穿的精确渐近结构，特别是当经典作用量呈对数增长导致能级劈裂呈现非整数幂耦合依赖时的解析机制。

Method: 将Razavy势映射为双合流Heun方程；在弱耦合极限下采用对数分离的Morse近似；利用波函数反射对称性分解谱条件；应用不规则奇异点处的精确WKB连接公式；用完全椭圆积分表示经典周期并计算首阶量子修正；对整数衰减指数情形实施Floquet模式收敛约化。

Conclusion: Razavy双势阱的非微扰劈裂由不规则奇点处的连接性质决定，其耦合依赖的非整数幂源于势垒作用量的对数发散；精确WKB与准精确可解性的协同可统一处理整数/非整数衰减指数情形，揭示了超对称量子力学与特殊函数理论在强非谐隧穿中的深层联系。

Abstract: We study nonperturbative level splitting in the symmetric Razavy hyperbolic double well, relating its double-confluent Heun connection problem to exact WKB and quasi-exact solvability. In the weak-coupling limit the wells separate logarithmically and approach two Morse systems. Reflection factorizes the two-ended spectral condition into separate even and odd quantization conditions. For each normalizable Morse level with noninteger decay exponent, the irregular-block connection formula yields the leading splitting with its full gamma-function coefficient, two analytic fluctuation corrections, and the leading logarithmic three-traversal contribution. The noninteger power of the coupling follows from the logarithmic growth of the barrier action. We express the classical periods in terms of complete elliptic integrals and derive the first quantum-period correction. At integer exponent, a convergent reduction onto the two resonant Floquet modes resolves the singular fixed-order expansion into two local spectral branches and reproduces the known finite-spin tunneling gap.

</details>


### [116] [The all-loop spectrum of GGK strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34619)
*Marius de Leeuw,Andrea Fontanella,Cameron Gibson,Juan Miguel Nieto García*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₅×S⁵中弦理论非相对论极限（GGK理论）的非微扰谱，在光锥与静态规范下，证明其玻色/费米谱由AdS₂中自由标量场精确描述；量子能修正经Hurwitz正则化后为零，故全谱仅由经典真空能量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证de Leeuw–Fontanella–Nieto-García关于GGK理论非微扰谱由AdS₂自由标量场描述的猜想，并统一处理玻色与费米自由度及不同规范下的量子一致性。

Method: 在光锥规范下分析多个真空的玻色谱；在静态规范下推广至任意经典弦真空并纳入费米自由度；采用正则化Hurwitz级数计算并抵消量子能修正。

Result: GGK理论在AdS₂中所有经典真空上的全谱（含费米子）由自由（无质量/有质量）标量场精确给出；量子能修正经Hurwitz正则化后严格为零。

Conclusion: GGK弦的量子能谱完全由其经典真空能量决定，不存在非平凡量子激发，表明该非相对论极限具有高度可解性与谱刚性。

Abstract: We study the non-perturbative spectrum of the non-relativistic limit of string theory in AdS$_5\times$S$^5$, also known as GGK theory. In light-cone gauge, we show that the bosonic spectrum on a number of vacua is described at all-loops by free massive and massless scalar fields in AdS$_2$, confirming a conjecture by de Leeuw, Fontanella and Nieto-García, arXiv:2403.09563. In static gauge, we obtain the same result for any classical string vacuum, and including fermions. We compute the quantum correction to the classical energy, and show that it vanishes once we adopt the regularised Hurwitz series. We conclude that the quantum energy spectrum built upon any GGK string consists solely of its classical vacuum energy.

</details>


### [117] [Interfaces in Long Range CFT$_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34705)
*Bastien Girault,Miguel F. Paulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₂中单个自由标量场理论的边界界面，涵盖Neumann/Dirichlet型界面、广义自由场与长程相互作用理论（如长程Ising）之间的界面，并在微扰论和边界共形bootstrap框架下计算了界面标度维数、g因子比及关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS₂中由边界相关算符诱导的共形界面的结构及其对全息对偶、边界CFT及量子信息的影响；建立可计算、可约束的界面物理可观测量体系。

Method: 结合解析共形映射、边界状态构造、微扰论（ε-展开与1/N展开）、数值共形bootstrap（针对四点界面关联函数）以及边界OPE分析。

Result: 精确给出Neumann/Dirichlet界面的标度维数Δₐ和g因子比gₙ/g𝒹；发展高效数值算法计算任意界面关联函数；在长程Ising等模型中导出界面数据的一阶微扰修正；通过bootstrap获得g因子比关于Δₐ的紧致上下界。

Conclusion: AdS₂中的标量界面具有丰富的共形结构，其可观测量（Δₐ, g/g₀）可被微扰与非微扰方法协同约束；界面bootstrap为低维全息界面提供严格检验框架。

Abstract: We consider interfaces in the theory of a single free scalar in AdS$_2$ defined by adding relevant operators along portions of the boundary. We begin with Neumann/Dirichlet interfaces, computing the associated scaling dimension and $g$-factor ratio; we also explain how arbitrary interface correlators can be computed numerically efficiently. Then we move on to interfaces between generalized free field and long-range interacting theories such as long range Ising, determining many quantities in perturbation theory. Finally we setup a bootstrap problem for four-point interface correlator. We use this to find tight bounds on the $g$-factor ratio as a function of the interface scaling dimension.

</details>


### [118] [Logarithmic scalar soft factorization from matching conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34791)
*Matías Briceño,Hernán A. González,Oriana Labrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了无质量标量场在Yukawa耦合下的对数软定理可由空间无穷远的渐近场方程与匹配条件导出，构造了满足对跖映射的守恒面积分，并统一描述其经典尾项与量子软辐射贡献，最终复现了一圈标量辐射的对数软系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无质量标量场缺乏规范对称性，无法像QED或引力那样通过渐近对称性直接导出对数软定理；本文旨在建立其软行为的内在动力学基础。

Method: 基于时空无穷远（i⁰、ℐ⁺、ℐ⁻）的渐近展开与匹配条件，从标量场的首阶对数衰减项构造守恒面积分；结合粒子轨迹计算经典辐射尾项，从渐近Dirac流提取硬荷；分离标量场解的经典特解与量子齐次解。

Result: 得到一个满足ℐ⁺与ℐ⁻间对跖映射的守恒量，其ℐ⁺上对应1/u辐射尾，ℐ⁻上对应入射粒子的非辐射对数结构；导出的Ward恒等式完整复现一圈标量发射的对数软系数（球面上零模除外）。

Conclusion: 标量场的对数软定理本质上源于渐近动力学约束而非规范对称性；经典与量子软贡献可在同一守恒框架下统一理解，支持软物理的普适几何结构观。

Abstract: Logarithmic soft theorems in QED and gravity can be understood as conservation laws associated with asymptotic symmetries. In contrast, for a massless scalar, no gauge symmetry is available to organize this relation. Despite that, we show that the logarithmic soft factorization for a massless scalar coupled to massive fermions through a Yukawa interaction follows from the asymptotic field equations and matching conditions at spatial infinity. From the first subleading logarithmic fall-off, we construct a conserved surface integral whose boundary values satisfy the antipodal map between past and future null infinity. At future null infinity, the field equations relate this quantity to the $1/u$ tail of the scalar radiation, while at past null infinity, for scattering states without incoming scalar radiation, it is carried by a non-radiative logarithm of the incoming particles. We derive the classical tail from the particle trajectories and obtain the hard charge from the asymptotic Dirac current. The particular solution of the leading scalar field gives the classical contribution, while its homogeneous component gives the quantum contribution. The resulting Ward identity reproduces the logarithmic soft coefficient of the one-loop scalar emission (up to a zero mode on the sphere), including its classical and quantum parts.

</details>


### [119] [Character expansion of the Itzykson-Zuber pair correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34829)
*Alexei Morozov,Hasib Sifat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了具有Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber测度的相关函数问题，目标是用Schur多项式的特征展开（character expansion）重现已知的两体关联函数结果，并利用Cauchy恒等式将无穷级数压缩为指数形式；关键创新在于用Cherednik算子构造的投影算子显式表达展开系数，为更复杂的酉群积分及非平凡测度下的高阶关联函数提供了新方法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建并深化对HCIZ测度下关联函数的理解，特别是以特征展开方式统一处理酉群积分——这类积分远比厄米情形困难，且现有方法难以推广至高阶或非平凡测度情形。

Method: 采用基于Schur函数的特征展开方法，结合Cauchy恒等式进行级数重求和，并引入Cherednik算子构造投影算子以精确控制展开系数。

Result: 成功将两体关联函数表示为带Cherednik投影算子调控系数的Schur多项式级数，并通过Cauchy恒等式实现指数化闭合表达；该结构为任意阶关联函数及更一般酉不变测度的计算提供了可推广的代数框架。

Conclusion: Cherednik算子与投影技术的引入，使HCIZ型酉积分的特征展开具备系统性与可扩展性，标志着向非平凡测度下高阶相关函数计算迈出关键一步。

Abstract: We revisit a long-standing problem of correlation functions with the Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber measure. The immediate goal is to reproduce the long-known answer for pair correlator in the form of character expansion - a sum over Schur polynomials with carefully adjusted coefficients. The infinite sum can be folded up into exponentials with the help of the Cauchy identity. What matters are the coefficients, which we express in terms of projectors, made from Cherednik operators. Development of character expansion methods for unitary integrals, which are much more difficult than the Hermitian ones, gives a hope for further generalizations - up to arbitrary correlators with non-trivial measures.

</details>
